Iska kaam ye hai ke mukhtalif AI Agents ko aik line (pipeline) mein lagaye taake wo mil kar aik bara kaam kar sakein. \n\nIs project mein ADK ye kaam kar raha hai:\n\n1.  **Sequential Pipeline:** Ye ensure karta hai ke pehlay **Planning Agent** kaam kary, phir uska output **Parser Agent** ko milay, phir wo data **Ranking Agent** ko jaye, aur aakhir mein **Reply Agent** ko.\n2.  **State Management:** ADK aik `context` handle karta hai.
